<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48086190' data-tags='Programming,Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/116550899908879585' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hardware Attestation as Monopoly Enabler</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>硬件认证成垄断推动者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48086190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2139</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48086190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 734</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hardware attestation mechanisms verify device state to software and remote services, enabling trusted-environment features. When the attestation is controlled by a single vendor, it can lock users into that ecosystem and hinder competition. The takeaway is a push for interoperable, transparent standards to prevent security from becoming a monopoly lever.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>硬件认证机制用于向软件和远程服务验证设备状态，支持可信执行环境等功能。若认证机制被单一厂商掌控，可能将用户锁定在该生态中，削弱竞争。核心信息是推动互操作、透明的认证标准，以防安全被用于垄断工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://grapheneos.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://grapheneos.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48085821' data-tags='Programming,AI,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unix.foo/posts/local-ai-needs-to-be-norm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Local AI needs to be the norm</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>本地 AI 应成为常态</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48085821' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1823</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48085821' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 727</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The push toward on-device, local AI is moving from novelty to necessity as privacy, latency, and data governance become real constraints for organizations. This shift demands tooling and standards for model updates, secure storage, and offline operation across devices and endpoints. Expect more investment in edge hardware, federated learning, and open models that respect user data sovereignty.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>向本地化 AI 迁移正在从新鲜事变成必要趋势，隐私、延迟和数据治理成为现实约束，促使在各设备端部署和离线运行模型。需要完善的更新、安全存储和跨设备协同的工具与标准。这将推动对边缘硬件、联邦学习以及尊重数据主权的开源模型的投入。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://unix.foo/images/brutalist_ios_main.png' data-full='https://unix.foo/images/brutalist_ios_main.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/bambu-lab-abusing-open-source-social-contract/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bambu Lab is abusing the open source social contract</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bambu Lab 滥用开源社会契约</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48109224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1173</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48109224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 378</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A strong critique argues Bambu Lab is deviating from the open-source social contract by locking down firmware or restricting community access to designs. The post frames this as part of a broader tension between consumer hardware startups and OSS norms, with potential implications for trust and ecosystem health.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇强烈的批评认为 Bambu Lab 偏离了开源社会契约，通过锁定固件或限制社区对设计的获取来压缩开源承诺。文中将此视为消费硬件初创公司与开源规范之间紧张关系的一部分，可能影响信任与生态系统健康。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48100706' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tanstack.com/blog/npm-supply-chain-compromise-postmortem' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Postmortem: TanStack NPM supply-chain compromise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TanStack npm 供应链安全事后分析</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48100706' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1069</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48100706' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 449</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A postmortem documents a TanStack npm supply-chain compromise, showing how a malicious change entered a published package and propagated to downstream projects. It outlines root causes, remediation efforts, and practical lessons for the npm ecosystem—emphasizing stronger access controls, monitoring, and package signing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>事后分析揭示 TanStack 的 npm 供应链被入侵，显示恶意变更如何进入已发布包并波及下游项目。文中给出根因、纠正措施，以及对 npm 生态的实际教训，强调加强访问控制、监控与包签名的重要性。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues for returning to hand-writing code to sharpen thinking, reduce tooling fatigue, and surface core problems before heavy IDE focus. The post offers practices that emphasize conceptual clarity over automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张回归手写代码，以提升思维清晰度、减少对工具的依赖，并更早地暴露本质问题。文章给出强调概念清晰度勝于过度自动化的实践建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medium.com/@NMitchem/if-ai-writes-your-code-why-use-python-bf8c4ba1a055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If AI writes your code, why use Python?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果 AI 编写你的代码，为什么还要用 Python？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48100433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 844</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48100433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 895</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take argues that if AI can write code, the rationale for choosing Python hinges less on convenience and more on ecosystem, governance, and performance considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇观点文章认为，当 AI 能代写代码时，选择 Python 的理由更多地来自生态系统、治理与性能等因素，而非单纯的开发便利。</p>

Highlights advantages for developers and power users, but cautions that accuracy can still slip and prompts require careful framing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分享了对 ChatGPT 5.5 Pro 的最新使用感受，指出响应更快、对话更连贯，长上下文处理也有所改善。强调这对开发者和高阶用户有实用价值，但仍需警惕输出的不准确性，并在使用时保持批判性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An incident report details a vulnerability tagged CVE-2024-YIKES and its exploitation path, affecting multiple systems before a patch was released. It highlights how attackers leveraged the flaw and the importance of rapid patching and layered defenses. The takeaway: keep dependencies current and strengthen incident response to reduce blast radius.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份事件报告披露了被标记为 CVE-2024-YIKES 的漏洞及其利用路径，影响了多套系统，直至修补程序发布。攻击者利用该漏洞进行入侵，凸显了快速打补丁和分层防御的重要性。关键点在于保持依赖项更新与强化事件响应以降低影响范围。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/jarredsumner/status/2053047748191232310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bun&#x27;s experimental Rust rewrite hits 99.8% test compatibility on Linux x64 glibc</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bun 的 Rust 重写在 Linux x64 glibc 上实现 99.8% 的测试兼容性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073680' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 697</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073680' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 675</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bun&#x27;s experimental Rust rewrite aims to improve startup and performance for the JavaScript runtime. Hitting 99.8% test compatibility on Linux x64 glibc signals strong parity with the current build, though a small gap remains. If realized, the rewrite could influence Bun&#x27;s future architecture and ecosystem adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bun 的实验性 Rust 重写旨在提升 JavaScript 运行时的启动速度与性能。在 Linux x64 glibc 上达到 99.8% 的测试兼容性，表明与现有实现的对齐性很高，但仍存在小幅差距。这一进展可能影响 Bun 的未来架构选择和生态系统的采用路径。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48166459' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.mozilla.org/netpolicy/2026/05/15/mozilla-to-uk-regulators-vpns-are-essential-privacy-and-security-tools-and-should-not-be-undermined/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mozilla to UK regulators: VPNs are essential privacy and security tools</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mozilla致英国监管机构：VPN是必需的隐私与安全工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48166459' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 689</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48166459' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 285</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla argues regulators should preserve VPNs as essential tools for privacy and security, outlining risks of weakening VPN access and potential impact on freedom of expression. It highlights the role of open standards and consumer rights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla 呼吁监管机构维护 VPN 的核心地位，强调其在隐私和安全方面的作用，同时警惕限制 VPN 访问对言论自由和安全的潜在影响。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://daniel.haxx.se/blog/2026/05/11/mythos-finds-a-curl-vulnerability/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mythos Finds a Curl Vulnerability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mythos 发现 curl 漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48091737' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 663</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48091737' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 275</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researcher Mythos has uncovered a vulnerability in curl, the widely used command-line HTTP client and library. The flaw underscores how even foundational networking tools can harbor exploitable weaknesses and reinforces the need for prompt patches and careful dependency management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mythos 发现 curl 的一个漏洞，curl 是广泛使用的 HTTP 客户端库。该漏洞凸显了基础网络组件也可能存在可被利用的弱点，强调及时修补和对依赖项进行谨慎管理的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://daniel.haxx.se/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/jinwon-robot.jpg' data-full='https://daniel.haxx.se/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/jinwon-robot.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A curated gallery of screenshots from classic desktop OSes highlighting UI design evolution, typography, and system chrome across decades. The collection offers quick insights into how desktop interfaces shaped user expectations and influenced modern OS design. A snapshot of how constraints shaped UX decisions and how designers balance clarity, affordances, and readability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>精选展示了经典桌面操作系统的屏幕截图，呈现跨年代的界面设计演变、排版与系统风格。通过这些历史画面，读者可以直观理解桌面交互如何塑造用户期望，并影响当代操作系统的设计。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ratty-term.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ratty – A terminal emulator with inline 3D graphics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ratty：一个支持内联 3D 图形的终端模拟器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48093100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 652</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48093100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 230</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ratty is a terminal emulator that renders inline 3D graphics, pushing terminal UX beyond plain text. It demonstrates how GPU-accelerated visuals can appear in developer tools, though practical use remains niche. A playful peek at how far terminal interfaces can be stretched.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ratty 是一款可在终端内直接显示三维图形的终端模拟器，突破了传统文本界面的局限。它展示了基于 GPU 的可视化在开发工具中的潜力，实际应用仍然有限。这个工具也算是一种推动终端界面边界的有趣尝试。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48092028' data-tags='Programming,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discuss.privacyguides.net/t/google-account-registration-now-requires-sending-an-sms-via-phone-instead-of-receiving-an-sms/36082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gmail registration now requires scanning a QR code and sending a text message</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gmail 注册现在需扫描二维码并发送短信</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48092028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 611</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48092028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 487</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New Gmail registration flows require scanning a QR code and sending an SMS to verify accounts. The change adds friction, raises privacy concerns, and may complic onboarding for new users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gmail 的注册流程现在要求先扫描二维码再发送短信完成验证。这一改变增加了注册难度，并引发隐私和无障碍方面的担忧，可能让新用户的上手变慢。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://forum-uploads.privacyguidesusercontent.com/original/3X/9/8/9831576af17cf56f7f57776620c4da4b4af14b7c.jpeg' data-full='https://forum-uploads.privacyguidesusercontent.com/original/3X/9/8/9831576af17cf56f7f57776620c4da4b4af14b7c.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>路易斯·罗斯曼愿为遭威胁的 OrcaSlicer 开发者支付诉讼费</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48084432' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 607</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48084432' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 325</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Louis Rossmann offers to cover legal fees for a threatened OrcaSlicer developer, signaling high-profile support for open hardware and repair activism. The gesture highlights ongoing tensions between manufacturers pursuing lawsuits and the enthusiast community defending rights to repair and independent tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>路易斯·罗斯曼愿为遭遇威胁的 OrcaSlicer 开发者支付诉讼费，显示对开放硬件与修理倡议的强力支持。这一举动凸显了厂商诉讼与热衷社区捍卫修理权和独立工具之间的持续紧张关系。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jola.dev/posts/running-local-models-on-m4' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Running local models on an M4 with 24GB memory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在配备 24GB 内存的 M4 上本地运行模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48089091' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 553</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48089091' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 172</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical exploration of running local AI models on an M4 with 24GB RAM, detailing the trade-offs between model size, quantization, and CPU inference. It shows how modest hardware can still enable useful local inference without cloud reliance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在配备 24GB 内存的 M4 上本地运行 AI 模型的实用探索，讨论模型大小、量化与 CPU 推理之间的权衡。展示了在相对有限的硬件上实现有用的本地推理、减少对云端的依赖。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://crates.io/crates/zerostack/1.0.0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zerostack – A Unix-inspired coding agent written in pure Rust</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48164287' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 546</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48164287' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 299</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zerostack is a Unix-inspired coding agent built in Rust, aiming to streamline command-line tooling and automation. It demonstrates how safety and performance-friendly Rust can empower developer tooling.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Software architecture is learned by building; this post outlines core concepts, common architectural styles, and a practical learning path. It emphasizes architecture as communication and tradeoff-making about quality attributes, with tips to study through real systems and examples.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过实践学习软件架构，这篇文章列出核心概念、常见架构风格，以及一个务实的学习路径。它强调架构是权衡取舍、关注质量属性并促进各方沟通的工具，给出通过案例和真实系统学习的办法。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48168221' data-tags='AI,Business,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://frederickvanbrabant.com/blog/2026-05-15-i-dont-think-ai-will-make-your-processes-go-faster/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I don&#x27;t think AI will make your processes go faster</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不认为AI会让你的流程更快</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 542</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 376</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that AI alone rarely speeds up human or team processes; gains depend on processes, governance, and integration work. It emphasizes thoughtful workflow redesign and measurable outcomes rather than chasing hype.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为单纯依赖 AI 很难直接让工作流程更快，成效取决于流程设计、治理与集成工作。强调在追赶热度前，先进行工作流再设计并设定可衡量的结果。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://frederickvanbrabant.com/images/posts/2026-05-15%20I%20don%27t%20think%20AI%20will%20make%20your%20processes%20go%20faster/header.jpg' data-full='https://frederickvanbrabant.com/images/posts/2026-05-15%20I%20don%27t%20think%20AI%20will%20make%20your%20processes%20go%20faster/header.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new adblocker project draws inspiration from the 1988 film They Live, likely using a visual filter concept to reveal or block ads in a novel way. It illustrates how pop-cultural ideas can spark fresh approaches to online privacy and UX, even if practical effectiveness varies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个受1988年电影《他们住着》启发的广告拦截器项目，以新颖的视觉过滤概念来拦截广告。该创意展示了流行文化如何激发网络隐私和用户体验的新思路，实际效果则需进一步评估。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nair.sh/guides-and-opinions/communicating-your-expertise/why-senior-developers-fail-to-communicate-their-expertise' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why senior developers fail to communicate their expertise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>资深开发者为何难以传达专业能力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48109460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 476</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48109460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 208</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Senior developers often fail to translate deep expertise into accessible guidance. The piece identifies gaps in storytelling, problem framing, and sharing decision processes, not just hard skills. It offers practical tactics like narrating workflows, giving reproducible examples, and clearly outlining tradeoffs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>资深开发者常常难以将深厚的专业知识转化为易于理解的指导。文章指出在讲故事、问题表述和分享决策过程等方面存在断层，而不仅仅是技能本身的问题，并给出通过讲解工作流、提供可复现示例以及清晰陈述权衡取舍等具体做法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nair.sh/_next/image?url=%2Fimages%2Fwsdftc%2Fwsdftc_cover.png&amp;w=3840&amp;q=75&amp;dpl=dpl_EJNiu3FBjkX8yJSwwGtmqoePQNU6' data-full='https://www.nair.sh/_next/image?url=%2Fimages%2Fwsdftc%2Fwsdftc_cover.png&amp;w=3840&amp;q=75&amp;dpl=dpl_EJNiu3FBjkX8yJSwwGtmqoePQNU6' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48106534' data-tags='Programming,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/05/12/tiktok-instagram-social-media-addictive-eu-crack-down.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU to crack down on TikTok, Instagram&#x27;s &#x27;addictive design&#x27; targeting kids</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟将打击 TikTok 与 Instagram 面向儿童的成瘾式设计</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48106534' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 453</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48106534' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 407</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EU plans tighter rules to curb addictive UX and data practices aimed at children on TikTok and Instagram, including enhanced age verification, limits on dark patterns, and tighter advertising controls. The move signals a broader push to regulate platform UX and data harvesting to protect minors while challenging social networks&#x27; monetization strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟拟对 TikTok 与 Instagram 的未成年人用户设计进行更严格的监管，限制成瘾式设计、加强年龄验证并收紧广告控制。此举标志着监管机构正在治理平台的用户体验与数据收集，对全球社媒公司盈利模式和合规成本都将产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108305441-1778573499669-gettyimages-2184717838-arriens-notitle241118_nprW7.jpeg?v=1778573506&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' data-full='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108305441-1778573499669-gettyimages-2184717838-arriens-notitle241118_nprW7.jpeg?v=1778573506&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48095550' data-tags='Career,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.seangoedecke.com/software-engineering-may-no-longer-be-a-lifetime-career/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Software engineering may no longer be a lifetime career</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>软件工程可能不再是终身职业</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48095550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 452</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48095550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 709</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that software engineering is increasingly a project-based, continuously upskilling career rather than a lifetime appointment. It suggests rethinking career models, education, and compensation to match a rapidly evolving tech landscape.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出软件工程越来越趋向以项目为基础、需要持续提升技能的职业模式，而非长期任职的“终身职业”。建议重新审视职业路径、教育与薪酬结构，以应对快速变化的技术环境。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.seangoedecke.com/og-image.jpg' data-full='https://www.seangoedecke.com/og-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>天空、日落与行星的渲染</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48107997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 443</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48107997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post dives into rendering the sky, sunsets, and planets, exploring artistic rendering techniques, color science, and performance considerations for web and UI graphics. It offers practical notes on achieving realistic celestial visuals while keeping rendering costs under control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章深入探讨在网页和界面图形中呈现天空、日落与行星的方法，覆盖色彩科学、光照模型和性能考量。给出在保持渲染成本可控的同时实现真实天体视觉效果的实用要点。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techspot.com/news/112410-security-researcher-microsoft-secretly-built-backdoor-bitlocker-releases.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Security researcher says Microsoft built a Bitlocker backdoor, releases exploit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>安全研究人员称微软秘密开发了 BitLocker 后门并发布漏洞利用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 437</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 177</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A researcher alleges a hidden BitLocker backdoor and has released an exploit demonstrating its feasibility. The claim, if true, would raise questions about OS-level encryption trust and supply-chain security, though verification and context are essential.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者声称发现 BitLocker 的隐藏后门并公布利用样例。如果属实，将带来对操作系统级别加密信任与供应链安全的质疑，但需要核实与上下文。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>UCLA 发现首个可修复脑损伤的中风康复药物（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48098261' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 425</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48098261' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>UCLA researchers report a breakthrough stroke rehabilitation drug capable of repairing brain damage, potentially improving recovery outcomes. If validated, it could reshape neurorehabilitation and complement existing therapies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州大学洛杉矶分校的研究团队宣布在中风康复药物方面取得突破，能够修复脑损伤并改善康复前景。若进一步证实，可能改变神经康复的治疗格局，并与现有疗法协同。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48158065' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 422</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48158065' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 394</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflective piece about complexity in technology and society, urging simplification or smarter tooling to avoid paralysis by analysis. It argues for principled design and focus.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>反思科技与社会中的复杂性，倡导简化和更聪明的工具，以避免分析瘫痪。主张以原则性设计与聚焦实现更有效的系统。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://justsitandgrin.im/posts/native-all-the-way-until-you-need-text/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Native all the way, until you need text</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>追求原生体验，直到你需要文本输入</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 410</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 274</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A debate about when to embrace native interfaces versus web/web-based solutions; native UX reigns for performance and feel, but text input and rich typography often push developers toward web tech. A prudent middle-ground emerges for pragmatic product design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>就原生界面与网页技术的取舍展开讨论：原生 UX 在性能与手感上更强，但文本输入和排版需求常使人转向网页技术。实用的设计取舍在于权衡场景与需求。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48096692' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nvlabs.github.io/cuda-oxide/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CUDA-oxide: Nvidia&#x27;s official Rust to CUDA compiler</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CUDA-oxide：NVIDIA 官方的 Rust 转 CUDA 编译器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48096692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 409</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48096692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA unveils CUDA-oxide, an official Rust-to-CUDA compiler that lets Rust code compile into CUDA kernels. The project aims to bring Rust&#x27;s safety and modern tooling to GPU programming, complementing CUDA C++. Adoption could accelerate Rust-based GPU workloads, but ecosystem, debugging, and performance comparisons will determine its traction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA 推出 CUDA-oxide，这是一款将 Rust 代码直接编译为 CUDA 内核的官方编译器。它借助 Rust 的安全性和现代工具链来改进 GPU 编程体验，作为 CUDA C++ 的补充。实际应用需要考量生态、调试和性能等权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nvlabs.github.io/cuda-oxide/_static/nvidia-logo-horiz-rgb-blk-for-screen.svg' data-full='https://nvlabs.github.io/cuda-oxide/_static/nvidia-logo-horiz-rgb-blk-for-screen.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48111896' data-tags='Programming,AI,Hardware,Show HN'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/cactus-compute/needle' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Needle: We Distilled Gemini Tool Calling into a 26M Model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Needle 将 Gemini 的工具调用压缩到 2600 万参数的模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48111896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 363</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48111896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Needle demonstrates that a tiny 26M parameter model can mimic Gemini&#x27;s tool-calling behavior by distilling how tool use works. The result suggests that compact models can perform practical agent-like tasks with proper prompting and training. This lowers the barrier to experimenting with AI agents on modest hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Needle 展示一个仅有 2600 万参数的模型，能够通过提炼工具调用模式来模拟 Gemini 的工具使用。该结果表明，在合适的提示和训练下，紧凑模型也能执行类似代理的实际任务，降低在中等硬件上的实验门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0f407ce30a671ff3fdb1af7b55c1709f4b5beda5dbaa9559a8ef6110db0618d1/cactus-compute/needle' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0f407ce30a671ff3fdb1af7b55c1709f4b5beda5dbaa9559a8ef6110db0618d1/cactus-compute/needle' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

The driver allegedly tracked a missing laptop to a state trooper&#x27;s house, underscoring tensions between forensics, privacy, and law enforcement. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名被控酒驾的司机据称通过追踪丢失笔记本的位置，找到了州警察的住处。这一事件暴露了取证与隐私之间的张力，并引发对日常科技数据来源与链路的讨论。</p>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.dochia.dev/blog/idempotency/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Idempotency is easy until the second request is different</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>幂等性：当第二次请求不同的时候变得困难</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48047930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 325</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48047930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 195</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the subtle pitfalls of idempotent APIs when the second request carries different state or intent. The post argues that ensuring true idempotence requires careful design, such as using immutable operations, deduplication, and compensating actions to keep systems consistent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章揭示幂等性在第二次请求若产生不同结果时的微妙陷阱。强调通过不可变操作、去重和补偿性行为等设计来维持系统的一致性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48170798' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stateofsurveillance.org/news/flock-cameras-destroyed-nationwide-ice-backlash-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Americans Are Smashing Flock Cameras</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国人全面拆除Flock监控摄像头</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48170798' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 323</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48170798' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A backlash against surveillance has fueled nationwide destruction of Flock cameras, highlighting tensions between privacy activists and city-wide security deployments. The incidents underscore how hardware choices and public policy intersect in the age of mass video data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对监控的强烈反对正在推动全国范围内拆除 Flock 摄像头，凸显隐私倡议者与城市监控部署之间的张力。此次事件暴露了硬件选择与公共政策在大规模视频数据时代的交叉作用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48159241' data-tags='Entertainment,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.antipope.org/charlie/blog-static/fiction/accelerando/accelerando.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Accelerando (2005)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Accelerando（2005）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48159241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 321</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48159241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 177</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Accelerando is a seminal techno-thriller about accelerating AI and posthuman futures; this edition or link likely presents a full text. It invites reflection on how rapid tech change reshapes society.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Accelerando》是关于加速AI与后人类未来的里程碑级科幻作品，文中探索科技快速演进如何改变社会结构与人类身份。提供对未来技术变迁的深刻反思。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48111531' data-tags='Programming,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/05/canadas-bill-c-22-repackaged-version-last-years-surveillance-nightmare' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Canada’s Bill C-22 Is a Repackaged Version of Last Year’s Surveillance Nightmare</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加拿大法案 C-22：再现去年监控噩梦的再包装</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48111531' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48111531' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bill C-22 reintroduces broad surveillance powers, sparking privacy concerns and calls for stronger oversight. Critics warn it mirrors last year’s worst surveillance practices and could chill digital rights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>C-22 法案重新引入广泛的监控权力，引发隐私担忧并呼吁加强监督。批评者警告其与去年的监控灾难性做法相似，可能压制数字权利。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/encryption-poc-chelsea-saunders.png' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/encryption-poc-chelsea-saunders.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48060077' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://slate.com/technology/2004/11/the-death-of-the-last-maverick-tech-company.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nullsoft, 1997-2004 (2004)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nullsoft：1997–2004 的最后一代独立科技公司</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48060077' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 316</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48060077' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nullsoft, the creator of Winamp and Shoutcast, epitomized late-90s indie software culture. It was acquired by AOL in 1999 for about $80 million, and the company faded as a standalone entity by 2004. The piece illustrates how consolidation reshaped the software landscape and marked the end of an era for Maverick tech studios.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nullsoft 的崛起伴随 Winamp 与 Shoutcast，被 AOL 于 1999 年收购，成为独立工作室的尾声。到 2004 年，作为独立实体的地位逐渐消退，这反映了科技行业的并购潮如何改变创新生态。它被视为那个时代最后一批“独行侠”型公司的终章。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://compote.slate.com/images/b58d1c35-93a6-4f0e-a689-756fe37b31c8.gif?width=1560' data-full='https://compote.slate.com/images/b58d1c35-93a6-4f0e-a689-756fe37b31c8.gif?width=1560' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48088151' data-tags='Energy,Politics,AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/artificial-intelligence/maryland-citizens-slapped-with-usd2-billion-grid-upgrade-bill-for-out-of-state-ai-data-centers-state-complains-to-federal-energy-regulators-says-additional-cost-breaks-ratepayer-protection-pledge-promises' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Maryland citizens hit with $2B power grid upgrade for out-of-state AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>因外州 AI 数据中心而来的电网升级让马里兰居民承担20亿美元成本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48088151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 316</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48088151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 200</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ratepayers face a multi-billion-dollar upgrade cost to support out-of-state AI data centers; regulators and lawmakers are challenging the billing and seeking protections. The dispute highlights how infrastructure costs for AI can be borne by residents rather than the benefiting entities. The outcome could influence policy on grid investments and fairness for ratepayers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为支持外州 AI 数据中心而进行的电网升级，导致用电者承担高达20亿美元的成本，引发监管机构与立法者的抗议与保护措施的讨论。这一分摊方式凸显了 AI 基础设施投资的公共成本问题，可能影响电网投资政策与对率费使用的公平性评估。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/KHUetaXQbsmm6z9m5g5Pne-2560-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/KHUetaXQbsmm6z9m5g5Pne-2560-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48115127' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/FULU-Foundation/OrcaSlicer-bambulab' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Restore full BambuNetwork support for Bambu Lab printers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为 Bambu Lab 打印机恢复对 BambuNetwork 的完整支持</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48115127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 314</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48115127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Community project to restore full BambuNetwork support for Bambu Lab printers in OrcaSlicer, enabling remote printing and network features. Highlights how open tooling extends hardware ecosystems beyond vendor limitations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个社区项目，旨在让 OrcaSlicer 重新全面支持 BambuLab 打印机的 BambuNetwork 功能，提升远程打印等网络能力。它体现了开源工具如何扩大硬件生态，超越厂商生态的局限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6b65e72a62cb1cb5135b6bea3d710f9595bf5469fbc2aeed04158a94d8423675/FULU-Foundation/OrcaSlicer-bambulab' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6b65e72a62cb1cb5135b6bea3d710f9595bf5469fbc2aeed04158a94d8423675/FULU-Foundation/OrcaSlicer-bambulab' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48163392' data-tags='Politics,AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/malta-chatgpt-plus-partnership/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI and Government of Malta partner to roll out ChatGPT Plus to all citizens</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 与马耳他政府合作向全体公民推出 ChatGPT Plus</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48163392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 313</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48163392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 320</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Malta&#x27;s government teams with OpenAI to roll out ChatGPT Plus to every citizen, signaling a push toward government-backed AI access and digital public services. The move raises questions about privacy, governance, and equal access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>马耳他政府与 OpenAI 合作，向所有公民推广 ChatGPT Plus，标志着政府支持的 AI 服务再临公共部门的数字化。此举引发隐私、治理与公平获取等讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/25iIHzkpMZU6D9guaSf5JD/fdbc74ef48943e248e832d406c68695c/SEO_Card__2_.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/25iIHzkpMZU6D9guaSf5JD/fdbc74ef48943e248e832d406c68695c/SEO_Card__2_.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48168198' data-tags='Hardware,Energy,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.williamangel.net/blog/2026/05/17/offline-llm-energy-use.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple Silicon costs more than OpenRouter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Apple Silicon 成本高于 OpenRouter</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168198' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 310</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168198' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comparison of energy efficiency and cost when running offline LLMs shows that consumer Apple Silicon may incur higher energy use or cost than purpose-built OpenRouter setups. Practical implications for on-device AI planning and hardware strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对离线运行的 AI 模型进行能耗与成本比较，表明 Apple Silicon 在离线推理上可能不如专用的 OpenRouter 设置高效或经济。为在设备端部署 AI 的硬件策略提供实际启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.williamangel.net/blog/2026/05/17/2026-05-17-offline-llm-energy-use.png.' data-full='https://www.williamangel.net/blog/2026/05/17/2026-05-17-offline-llm-energy-use.png.' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48168056' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thestateofbrand.com/news/ai-subscription-time-bomb' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Every AI Subscription Is a Ticking Time Bomb for Enterprise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>每个AI订阅都可能成为企业的定时炸弹</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 306</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 302</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that AI subscription models can create cost unpredictability and vendor lock-in for enterprises, urging clearer governance, exit options, and budget planning to mitigate risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出 AI 订阅模型可能带来成本不可预测、厂商绑定等风险，呼吁加强治理、设定退出策略并进行预算规划以降低隐性负担。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/69d68fe7dde9e79d19ddf151/6a02492bc7c745f849372fe4_364ffc2a-1a23-423f-b62a-3764e12ce6ab.webp' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/69d68fe7dde9e79d19ddf151/6a02492bc7c745f849372fe4_364ffc2a-1a23-423f-b62a-3764e12ce6ab.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48168668' data-tags='Hardware,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/tech4bot/rk3562deb' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I turned a $80 RK3562 Android tablet into a Debian Linux workstation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把 80 美元的 RK3562 Android 平板改造成 Debian 工作站</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 299</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A budget-friendly hack demonstrates how a low-end Android tablet can become a functional Debian workstation, shedding light on hardware limits, driver support, and the potential for repurposing consumer devices. It shows that with the right setup, cheap hardware can become viable for light development tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用一块低价平板就能跑 Debian，展示了硬件极限、驱动与开源生态对再利用的影响。通过合适的设置，廉价设备也能胜任轻量开发工作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0ea6e6a451fcaaa0d5e1b6e7cea66fed8546d35a4c6c45a53913012782160c3b/tech4bot/rk3562deb' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0ea6e6a451fcaaa0d5e1b6e7cea66fed8546d35a4c6c45a53913012782160c3b/tech4bot/rk3562deb' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48093043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 250</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48093043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 185</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As AI-powered note-taking tools proliferate in law firms and courts, lawyers worry about client confidentiality, privilege, and the risk of hallucinations or misattribution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人工智能笔记工具在律师事务所和法庭越来越普及，引发对客户保密、特权以及信息泄露风险的担忧。文章讨论了相关的政策、数据处理与伦理准则如何演进，以及这类工具对计费和策略的潜在影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48103668' data-tags='Programming,Security,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.insidehighered.com/news/tech-innovation/administrative-tech/2026/05/11/instructure-pays-ransom-canvas-hackers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Instructure pays ransom to Canvas hackers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Instructure 向 Canvas 黑客支付赎金</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48103668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 249</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48103668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 235</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Instructure reportedly paid ransom to attackers who breached Canvas, illustrating the ongoing ransomware risk in education tech. The decision underscores the tension between public disclosure, downtime costs, and the ethics of paying attackers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称 Instructure 已向入侵 Canvas 的攻击者支付赎金，凸显教育技术领域持续的勒索软件风险。此举揭示了公开披露、系统停机成本与付费给攻击者之间的伦理与权衡。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cocoawithlove.com/blog/matrix-multiplications-swift.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Training an LLM in Swift, Part 1: Taking matrix mult from Gflop/s to Tflop/s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Swift 中训练大语言模型（第一部分）：把矩阵乘法性能从 GFLOP/s 提升到 TFLOP/s</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48085685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 249</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48085685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This post kicks off a series on training an LLM in Swift, focusing on pushing matrix-multiplication throughput from GFLOP/s toward TFLOP/s. It digs into practical low-level optimizations, potential backends like Accelerate or Metal, and the scale challenges of pretraining in Swift.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本帖开启用 Swift 训练大语言模型的系列，聚焦把矩阵乘法的吞吐量从 GFLOP/s 提升到 TFLOP/s。内容涉及低级优化、可用的后端（如 Accelerate/Metal）以及在 Swift 生态中实现大规模预训练所面临的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.cocoawithlove.com/assets/site/touch_heartandcup.png' data-full='https://www.cocoawithlove.com/assets/site/touch_heartandcup.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48081469' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://g5t.de/articles/20260510-task-paralysis-and-ai/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Task Paralysis and AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>任务瘫痪与人工智能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48081469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 248</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48081469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece analyzes how AI assistants can induce task paralysis by outsourcing cognitive steps, causing users to hesitate and overplan. It offers strategies such as decomposing tasks, incremental prompts, and minimal viable outputs to maintain momentum.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章分析了 AI 助手通过外包认知步骤可能引发的任务瘫痪，使用户犹豫并过度规划。提供将任务拆解、分步提示和产出最小可行结果等策略以保持推进。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://duckdb.org/2026/05/12/quack-remote-protocol' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Quack: The DuckDB Client-Server Protocol</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Quack：DuckDB 的客户端-服务器协议</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48111765' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 238</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48111765' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Quack is DuckDB&#x27;s remote protocol designed to streamline client-server integration, enabling efficient SQL queries over a standardized interface. It aims to simplify language bindings and enable streaming results for analytics workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Quack 是 DuckDB 的远程协议，旨在简化客户端-服务器集成，通过标准接口高效执行 SQL 查询，并支持流式结果，便于分析工作负载的跨语言绑定。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google disclosed that criminals used AI-powered tools to locate a major software flaw, illustrating how AI accelerates vulnerability discovery and exploitation. The revelation underscores the urgency of rapid patching and proactive security monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌披露，犯罪黑客利用 AI 来发现一处重大软件漏洞，凸显 AI 在漏洞发现与利用方面的提速效应。该披露强调快速打补丁和主动安全监控的紧迫性。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>delta-mem proposes an efficient online memory mechanism to support large language model inference, potentially reducing memory footprints and enabling longer context windows. It signals ongoing research into scalable memory for LLMs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>δ-mem 提出了一种面向大语言模型推理的高效在线内存机制，可能降低内存占用并扩展上下文长度。显示出针对 LLM 可扩展内存的持续研究。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48165980' data-tags='Energy,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://electrek.co/2026/05/14/tesla-solar-roof-promise-vs-reality-pivot-panels/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla Solar Roof is on life support as it pivot to panels</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉太阳能屋顶陷入生死边缘，转而聚焦太阳能板</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48165980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48165980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 218</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tesla Solar Roof is on life support as it pivots to traditional panels; the premium roof product has faced cost and installation hurdles, pushing the company to focus on conventional PV. This shift may accelerate adoption of standard panels and reshape expectations for building-integrated photovoltaics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特斯拉太阳能屋顶在成本与安装难题面前陷入难题，转而重点推进传统太阳能板。此举或将加速传统面板的普及，并改变建筑一体化光伏的市场预期。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the realm of extremely low-frequency signals and the hardware and physics behind them. From submarine communications to natural geophysical phenomena, these frequencies pose unique challenges for transmission, sensing, and timing. The piece sheds light on why engineers care about ELF in both military and scientific contexts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>聚焦极低频段信号的物理与技术特征。极低频在潜艇通信等场景以及地球物理现象中扮演独特角色，带来传输、探测和时间同步方面的挑战。文章阐释了为何 ELF 技术在军事与科学领域仍具吸引力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48085330' data-tags='Energy,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://janrosenow.substack.com/p/spain-just-became-one-of-europes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spain has become one of Europe’s cheapest power markets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西班牙成为欧洲最便宜的电力市场之一</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48085330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 183</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48085330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spain has emerged as one of Europe&#x27;s cheapest power markets, driven by ample renewables and favorable pricing dynamics. The trend could attract data centers and energy-intensive industries, but may also raise concerns about price volatility and grid investments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西班牙凭借丰富的可再生能源和有利的定价结构，成为欧洲最便宜的电力市场之一。这一趋势可能吸引数据中心与高耗能行业，但也可能带来价格波动与电网投资方面的担忧。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Revisiting the Hindenburg&#x27;s smoking room shows how smoking aboard reflected its era&#x27;s norms and safety limits for hydrogen airships. The piece uses this detail to illuminate design trade-offs and the broader context of the disaster.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾兴登堡号上的吸烟室，揭示了当时社会对吸烟的态度以及氢气飞艇的安全挑战。文章以这一细节为切入点，阐释设计取舍及灾难发生的背景。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/05/eff-fourth-circuit-electronic-device-searches-border-require-warrant' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EFF to 4th Circuit: Electronic Device Searches at the Border Require a Warrant</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>EFF 向第四巡回法院：边境电子设备搜查须获搜查令</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48115059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48115059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EFF argues that border searches of electronic devices require a warrant to protect digital privacy. Highlights implications for travelers and ongoing privacy-rights debates in immigration enforcement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EFF 主张边境对电子设备的搜查应当获得搜查令，以保护数字隐私。文章讨论旅行者的隐私权和移民执法中的权利界限。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48096842' data-tags='Programming,Science,Show HN'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://andreaturchet.github.io/website/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: TikTok but for scientific papers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：面向科学论文的 TikTok 式应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48096842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48096842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: TikTok but for Scientific Papers introduces a vertical-video style platform for science papers, summarizing research in bite-sized clips to accelerate discovery. It taps into the trend of short-form content to help researchers skim literature, but raises questions about quality control and fragmentation of detail. Community feedback and curation will be key to balancing speed with credibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN：面向科学论文的 TikTok 式应用将科学论文以竖屏短视频形式呈现，帮助研究者快速浏览并理解论文要点。此举顺应了短视频传播的趋势，但也引发对内容质量与细节完整性的担忧。社区的共审与内容筛选将成为关键。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://andreaturchet.github.io/website/assets/papel-logo.png' data-full='https://andreaturchet.github.io/website/assets/papel-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48084836' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://e360.yale.edu/features/amoc-climate-change' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scientists warn Atlantic current at risk of shutting down</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科学家警告大西洋经向环流面临关闭风险</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48084836' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48084836' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 227</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scientists warn that the Atlantic Meridional Overturning Circulation could slow or shut down as climate change progresses. A slowdown would reshape regional climates, potentially altering weather patterns in Europe, North America, and beyond.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科学家警告，在气候变化推动下，大西洋经向翻转环流（AMOC）可能减弱甚至关闭。若发生，其将改变欧洲和美洲等地区的天气模式及海洋生态，需要尽早采取减排等应对措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://yale-threesixty.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/AMOC_NASA-HEADER.jpg?w=1200&amp;q=60&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;dm=1777998975&amp;s=166da29d752298535aeff0ec87e19e2b' data-full='https://yale-threesixty.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/AMOC_NASA-HEADER.jpg?w=1200&amp;q=60&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;dm=1777998975&amp;s=166da29d752298535aeff0ec87e19e2b' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/ucf-ai-commencement-speaker-booed/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Students boo commencement speaker after she calls AI next industrial revolution</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>学生在致辞称 AI 将成为下一场工业革命时被嘘声</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48096674' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48096674' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 200</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>During a university commencement, students booed a speaker after she framed AI as the next industrial revolution. The moment captures the ongoing tension around AI hype, jobs, and the culture war on campus.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在毕业典礼上，一位演讲者将 AI 称为下一场工业革命，随后遭到学生嘘声。事件折射出校园对 AI 热潮、就业影响及相关伦理议题的分歧与紧张。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/05/ucf.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/05/ucf.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48168485' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/alternbits/awesome-cuda-books' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CUDA Books</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CUDA 书籍合集</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A curated round-up of CUDA-focused books for developers, outlining topics from fundamentals to advanced GPU programming. It serves as a quick learning path for accelerating workloads on GPUs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>收集整理了专注于 CUDA 的书籍，涵盖从入门到高级的 GPU 编程主题，为加速工作负载提供了快速学习路径。</p>
