<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tautme.github.io/phone-sensors/accel-tuner.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Guitar tuner that uses phone accelerometer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于手机加速度计的吉他调音器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48058260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48058260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A guitar tuner built around the phone&#x27;s accelerometer demonstrates how inertial sensing can substitute audio capture for instrument tuning. By measuring vibrations via the accelerometer, the app can offer low-latency tuning and resilience to noisy environments, showcasing a novel use of everyday sensors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个吉他调音器利用手机的加速度计来侦测弦振动，展示了惯性传感在调音任务中的另类应用。通过测量振动，该方法可能实现更低延迟的调音并在嘈杂环境中更稳健，体现了日常传感器在音乐工具中的潜力。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MathOverflow hosts a question about what a mathematician should do to maximize impact and career prospects. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个关于数学家的职业路径的 MathOverflow 问题引发广泛讨论。回复强调聚焦清晰的问题、建立合作关系、将研究成果更广泛地传播，以及在理论深度与潜在应用之间寻求平衡。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/mexican-science-transforms-scorpion-venom-and-habanero-chile-into-antibiotics-against-resistant-bacteria/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Venom and hot peppers offer a key to killing resistant bacteria</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蝎毒与哈瓦那辣椒为对抗耐药细菌提供关键线索</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48070571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 159</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48070571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scientists show that combining scorpion venom-derived compounds with habanero pepper chemistry can kill antibiotic-resistant bacteria, offering a potential new class of anti-infectives. While promising, the approach awaits clinical validation and safety assessment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究将蝎毒成分与哈瓦那辣椒化合物结合，成功杀死耐药细菌，为新型抗菌药物提供潜在路径。尽管前景看好，但仍需要临床验证和安全评估。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://interfaze.ai/blog/interfaze-a-new-model-architecture-built-for-high-accuracy-at-scale' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Interfaze: A new model architecture built for high accuracy at scale</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Interfaze：为大规模场景打造的高精度新模型架构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48097078' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48097078' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Interfaze introduces a new model architecture designed to deliver high accuracy at scale, addressing the trade-offs between model size, training cost, and performance. The approach purportedly enables reliable, scalable deployment for large datasets and real-time inference. If proven, it could influence how enterprises plan AI systems for production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Interfaze 提出一种新型模型架构，旨在在大规模场景中保持高精度，权衡了模型规模、训练成本与性能。该方法声称能在大数据集上实现更可靠的可扩展性与实时推理。若落地，将影响企业在生产层面的 AI 系统设计与部署策略。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mamba-studio/TypedMemory' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Library for fast mapping of Java records to native memory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用于将 Java 记录快速映射到本机内存的库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48099616' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48099616' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TypedMemory provides zero-copy mapping of Java records into native memory, enabling off-heap data structures and potential GC relief for JVM apps. It targets high-performance workloads where minimal overhead matters.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TypedMemory 提供将 Java 记录零拷贝映射到本机内存的能力，支持离堆数据结构并改善 GC 压力，适用于对性能敏感的 JVM 场景。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.arup.com/en-us/news/first-fehmarnbelt-tunnel-element-lowered/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>First tunnel element of the Fehmarnbelt Tunnel immersed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>费姆恩贝尔特隧道首个隧道单元下沉完成</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48050666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 150</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48050666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The first tunnel element of the Fehmarnbelt fixed link has been lowered into place, marking a milestone in one of Europe’s largest infrastructure projects. The operation demonstrates precision engineering at scale and advances a cross‑Baltic rail and road corridor expected to boost regional freight and mobility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>费姆恩贝尔特海底隧道的首个隧道单元已成功下沉，标志着全球最大规模基础设施项目之一的关键里程碑。这一作业展示了大规模精密工程，并将推动横跨波罗的海的铁路与公路走廊，提升区域货运与出行能力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48080702' data-tags='AI,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/expanded-gemini-api-file-search-multimodal-rag/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemini API File Search is now multimodal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini API 文件搜索现已支持多模态</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48080702' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 147</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48080702' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Gemini API file search now supports multimodal inputs, enabling richer queries over documents and media with retrieval-augmented generation. This broadens how developers build search interfaces on Gemini and signals a trend toward multimodal tooling in AI APIs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemini API 文件搜索现已支持多模态输入，允许对文档和多媒体内容进行更丰富的查询，并结合检索增强的生成能力。此举拓展了开发者在 Gemini 上构建搜索界面的方式，体现了 AI API 多模态工具的发展趋势。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://finance.yahoo.com/markets/stocks/articles/590-000-buyers-paid-59-223500998.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>590k buyers paid $59M for Trump&#x27;s gold phone, but not one has shipped</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特朗普的金色手机：59万买家支付约5900万美元，但尚未发货</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48100152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 144</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48100152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 103</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Despite hundreds of thousands of buyers signing up and roughly $59 million in payments, the Trump-branded gold phone has yet to ship. The episode illustrates the gap between flashy marketing and execution in celebrity-branded hardware ventures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管约有59万买家支付了约5900万美元，但特朗普品牌金色手机尚未发货。此事揭示名人跨界硬件营销的 hype 与落地执行之间的差距。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s.yimg.com/ny/api/res/1.2/cvQAv8WdakuPFkoOTCvWDQ--/YXBwaWQ9aGlnaGxhbmRlcjt3PTEyMDA7aD02NzY-/https://media.zenfs.com/en/moneywise_327/af42ee49a29b8113b4b0703486b0c22f' data-full='https://s.yimg.com/ny/api/res/1.2/cvQAv8WdakuPFkoOTCvWDQ--/YXBwaWQ9aGlnaGxhbmRlcjt3PTEyMDA7aD02NzY-/https://media.zenfs.com/en/moneywise_327/af42ee49a29b8113b4b0703486b0c22f' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/payphone-voip' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>VoIP brings back old-fashioned pay phones to rural Vermont (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>VoIP 技术在佛蒙特州农村重新部署电话亭（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48172505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48172505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A pilot uses VoIP-based payphones to fill gaps in rural Vermont where mobile coverage is spotty. Internet-connected kiosks offer emergency calling and everyday use, reviving a classic public amenity with modern networking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>VoIP 技术在佛蒙特州农村重新部署电话亭，弥补移动覆盖薄弱带来的通信盲区。借助互联网连接，这些亭子既能提供紧急呼叫，也能完成日常通话，展示用现代网络改造旧有基础设施的尝试。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48065429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 140</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48065429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece examines Amália and the future of European Portuguese LLMs, arguing that European Portuguese deserves tailored data, benchmarks, and governance to avoid dominant models erasing regional variation. It discusses linguistic diversity, data accessibility, and policy considerations shaping EU NLP.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨 Amália 与欧洲葡萄牙语大型语言模型的未来，强调需要针对欧洲葡萄牙语的数据、评估指标和治理框架，避免出现压倒性主流模型而边缘化地区变体的情况。讨论语言多样性、数据获取与推动欧洲 NLP 的政策要素。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/health/wellness/hearing-loss-walking-speed-iphone-study-c53c482a' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Walking slower? Your ears, not your knees, might be the problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>走路变慢？耳朵可能是关键，而非膝盖</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48075429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 135</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48075429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study links age-related hearing loss to slower walking speed, suggesting auditory health impacts mobility in older adults. Using data from a large-scale smartphone-based study, researchers show a notable association that could motivate early hearing interventions to preserve independence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项研究将与年龄相关的听力损失与步速减慢联系起来，表明听力健康会影响老年人的行动能力。基于大规模手机数据的研究显示出显著关联，可能推动及早听力干预以维持独立性。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48061602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48061602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide to preventing MITM on the very first SSH connection across clouds and VPS providers. It emphasizes out-of-band host key verification, pinning, and robust initial trust strategies to reduce supply-chain-like risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供在首次 SSH 连接时防止 MITM 攻击的实用指南，适用于任意云与 VPS 提供商。强调带外验证、主机密钥固定以及稳健的初始信任策略，以降低类似供应链的风险。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c202pgxx89lo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US inflation jumps to 3.8% as energy costs surge from Iran war</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国通胀因能源成本上升至3.8%（与伊朗冲突相关）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48108313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48108313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>US inflation rose to 3.8% as energy costs spiked due to the Iran war, reinforcing concerns about cost of living and monetary policy. The uptick can influence consumer tech spending and corporate pricing strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>能源价格在与伊朗战争相关的冲突中走高，推动美国通胀上升至3.8%。这一数据可能影响家庭消费和企业定价，进而影响科技硬件及云服务等领域的支出走向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/f750/live/8172ffc0-4e00-11f1-aa37-6b8846c2f74e.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/f750/live/8172ffc0-4e00-11f1-aa37-6b8846c2f74e.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Killed by Apple chronicles how Apple’s platform decisions have erased products, apps, and ecosystems, highlighting platform owners&#x27; power to reshape markets. It frames the ongoing debate over openness versus control in tech ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Killed by Apple》聚焦苹果对生态的强力控制如何让一些产品、应用和服务被迫退出。它揭示了平台拥有者对市场的强大塑造力，以及围绕开放性与封闭生态的长期辩论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.databricks.com/blog/how-lakebase-architecture-delivers-5x-faster-postgres-writes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lakebase architecture delivers faster Postgres writes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lakebase 架构让 Postgres 写入速度提升 5 倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48064789' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48064789' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Databricks’ Lakebase architecture promises a fivefold improvement in PostgreSQL write throughput by combining lakehouse data management with optimized storage and compute separation. This could reshape data pipelines for analytics workloads, but adoption will hinge on compatibility and operational complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lakebase 架构通过整合数据湖管理、存储与计算分离等技术，承诺将 PostgreSQL 的写入吞吐提升约五倍。这可能重塑分析型工作负载的数据管线，但实际落地取决于与现有系统的兼容性与运维复杂性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.databricks.com/sites/default/files/2026-05/2025-10-blog-lakebase-fully-managed-postgres-database-integrated-with-databricks-og-1200x628-2x.png' data-full='https://www.databricks.com/sites/default/files/2026-05/2025-10-blog-lakebase-fully-managed-postgres-database-integrated-with-databricks-og-1200x628-2x.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://link.springer.com/article/10.1007/s42113-026-00271-1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Illusions of understanding in the sciences</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科学中的理解幻觉</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48140297' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48140297' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A discussion of how scientists can misinterpret understanding, highlighting cognitive biases, overgeneralization, and the gap between models and explanations. It urges humility and better epistemic practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨科学家可能产生的理解错觉，指出认知偏差、泛化过度及模型与解释之间的差距。倡导更谦逊、更健全的知识获取方法。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(138) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.aea3321' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A molecule with half-Möbius topology</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>具有半 Möbius 拓扑的分子</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48109756' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48109756' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Science reports a molecule with half-Möbius topology, a rare topological structure that could yield unusual electronic and optical properties, with implications for molecular electronics and materials science.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科学家报道一种具有半 Möbius 拓扑的分子，这种罕见拓扑结构可能带来不同寻常的电子和光学性质，对分子电子学与材料科学具有潜在影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>考古学家发现一段失落的古代文字，为书写作为符号体系的起源提供线索。此发现帮助我们理解从原始符号到正式文字的演化，以及 literacy 所依赖的社会技术。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Prolog has a reputation for a steep learning curve and quirky behavior that makes debugging a headache. This piece highlights Prolog&#x27;s backtracking and logic-first mindset, arguing many developers find it a painful fit for real-world projects.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://andrealeopardi.com/posts/erlang-counters-and-atomics/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Counting Fast in Erlang with:counters and:atomics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Erlang 中的快速计数：使用 counters 与 atomics</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Erlang in practice shows how to count fast using counters and atomics, enabling high-throughput counting without sacrificing BEAM&#x27;s concurrency model. The article likely demonstrates patterns for safe, lock-free increments and shared counters, including ETS-based approaches. These techniques help write scalable, low-latency services in Erlang.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章展示了在 Erlang 中使用 counters 与 atomics 实现高速计数的方法，兼顾并发模型。通过无锁增量和共享计数器的模式，以及基于 ETS 的实现，提升了吞吐与响应速度。对于构建可扩展、低延迟的 Erlang 服务很有参考价值。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48075131' data-tags='Programming,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/pion/rtwatch' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rtwatch: Watch videos with friends using WebRTC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rtwatch：用 WebRTC 与朋友一起观看视频</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48075131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48075131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rtwatch enables synchronized video watching with friends using WebRTC, enabling private, peer-to-peer co-viewing without a heavy CDN. It coordinates playback across peer connections and handles basic chat or controls through the same WebRTC stack. This approach highlights the growing interest in peer-to-peer collaboration tools for media and privacy-preserving experiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rtwatch 让用户通过 WebRTC 实现与朋友的同步视频观看，支持私密的点对点共看，避免依赖集中式媒体服务器。它在对等连接间协调播放并通过同一对等通信栈处理简单的互动。此类做法体现了在媒体协作中对隐私与低延迟体验的日益关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1290b761c2c469993c115eb5a8f1230914b5b60f764cd768c744f9876160c406/pion/rtwatch' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1290b761c2c469993c115eb5a8f1230914b5b60f764cd768c744f9876160c406/pion/rtwatch' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new driver enables Griffin PowerMate knob to work on modern macOS versions, reviving a vintage volume control with updated OS APIs. It highlights the importance of community-maintained drivers for legacy peripherals and the friction users face when hardware support lags OS updates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个新驱动让 Griffin PowerMate 旋钮在现代 macOS 上重新工作，利用更新的系统 API 实现体积控制等功能。此举凸显社区维护驱动的重要性，以及操作系统更新对旧外设支持的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/ai-artificial-intelligence/644853/pew-gallup-data-americans-dont-trust-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Most Americans don&#x27;t trust AI – or the people in charge of it (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大多数美国人不信任AI——以及掌管它的人（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48174808' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48174808' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New Pew/Gallup data show Americans&#x27; trust in AI and the people regulating it remains low, with trust varying by demographics and political affiliation. Increasing transparency and accountability could shift trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新的皮尤/盖洛普数据表明，美国人对 AI 及其监管者的信任度仍然偏低，信任程度随人群和政治立场而异。提升透明度与问责制或许会提升公众信任。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>洛杉矶的新地铁站可能成为改变城市交通格局的关键举措，旨在缓解拥堵、推动沿线区域的发展，并减少对私家车的依赖。报道也指出资金、政治意愿与公平性等挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A write-up demonstrates how 16 bytes of x86 code can synthesize audible sound from the Matrix rain visualization, illustrating extreme code-golf techniques and low-level audio synthesis. The result highlights how tiny snippets can produce multimedia.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇写作展示了用仅仅16字节的 x86 代码就能把矩阵雨可转成声音，体现了极简编码与多媒体合成的极限。对于低级编程与代码高球爱好者而言，这是一个有趣的演示，展示了紧凑代码如何实现音频合成。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lanzaboote covers enabling Secure Boot on NixOS, detailing steps for key management, signing kernels and modules, and ensuring chain-of-trust in a Nix-based system. The piece helps NixOS users secure their boot process in environments where firmware trust is critical.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lanzaboote 介绍在 NixOS 上实现安全引导的要点，包括密钥管理、内核与模块签名、以及在 Nix 基础架构中保持启动可信链的做法，帮助在固件信任至关重要的环境中提升安全性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dead.Letter（CVE-2026-45185）——XBOW 发现 Exim 的未认证 RCE</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48111748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48111748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Exim remote code execution vulnerability CVE-2026-45185 is unauthenticated, allowing attackers to take over mail servers without credentials. XBOW details how the flaw was discovered and remediation steps practitioners should apply.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Exim 的远程代码执行漏洞 CVE-2026-45185 未经认证，攻击者可在未提供凭据的情况下控制邮件服务器。XBOW 说明了该漏洞的发现过程，并给出实务中的修复步骤。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>VGA 内存访问很复杂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48076798' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48076798' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The OS2Museum post demystifies VGA memory access, showing how legacy VGA addressing, banking, and color planes complicate low-level graphics on old hardware. It’s a reminder that even decades-old interfaces carry non-obvious quirks that modern abstractions often hide.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OS2Museum 的文章解读了 VGA 内存访问的复杂性，揭示了旧硬件上的寻址、银行切换和颜色平面等要素的难点。提醒人们即便是几十年前的接口也有不直观的细节，现代抽象往往掩盖了这些问题。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.osnews.com/story/144943/eu-weighs-restricting-use-of-us-cloud-platforms-to-process-sensitive-government-data/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU weighs restricting use of US cloud platforms to process sensitive gov data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟考虑限制对敏感政府数据使用美国云平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48171015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48171015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EU is weighing rules to keep sensitive government data off US cloud platforms, signaling rising data sovereignty concerns and potential compliance hurdles for vendors. This could accelerate Europe-built or privacy-preserving alternatives and reshape cloud procurement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟正考虑将敏感政府数据限制在美国云平台之外，标志着对数据主权的日益关注以及对厂商的合规挑战。此举可能推动欧洲自建或隐私保护的替代方案，影响云服务采购。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48056639' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thenarwhal.ca/bird-banding-ontario/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The people preserving the scientific practice of bird banding</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>守护鸟类环志科学实践的人们</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48056639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48056639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ontario bird banding communities keep a long-running scientific practice alive, collecting migration data through careful tagging and observation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安大略省的鸟类环志社群维持着一项长期科学实践，通过细致的标记和观察收集迁徙数据。文章聚焦志愿者与研究者如何维持田野方法、培养新参与者，以及数据质量在生态研究中的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48074589' data-tags='Programming,Hardware,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zxbasic.readthedocs.io/en/docs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Boriel BASIC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Boriel BASIC 编程语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48074589' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48074589' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Boriel BASIC provides a cross-compiler and tooling to run BASIC on Z80-based systems, reviving classic programming workflows with modern development conveniences. It targets ZX Spectrum and other retro platforms, enabling enthusiasts to compile and run old-school BASIC programs on contemporary hardware. The project exemplifies retrocomputing tooling that keeps historic microcomputing accessible for education and experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Boriel BASIC 提供面向 Z80 系列平台的 BASIC 跨编译器与开发工具，帮助复兴经典编程流程，并带来现代化的开发便利。它支持 ZX Spectrum 等复古平台，使爱好者能够在当代硬件上编译并运行早期的 BASIC 程序。该项目体现了让历史性微型计算保持可访问性的复古计算工具链，适用于教育与实验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.ko-fi.com/img/githubbutton_sm.svg' data-full='https://www.ko-fi.com/img/githubbutton_sm.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.andymasley.com/p/the-ai-water-issue-is-fake' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The AI water issue is fake</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI用水问题其实是假的</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48171826' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48171826' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece disputes claims about AI&#x27;s water footprint, arguing that water use is not the dominant constraint and that efficiency improvements, localized contexts, and data center innovation matter more. It encourages nuance over alarmist narratives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章质疑关于 AI 用水量的说法，认为水资源并非主要瓶颈，能源与数据中心的效率提升、区域情境才是关键。提倡对话中避免危言耸听，关注细节与改进空间。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!EtUU!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fdcf8b781-d102-493b-bb7d-17568424fa9e_1456x816.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!EtUU!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fdcf8b781-d102-493b-bb7d-17568424fa9e_1456x816.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48116064' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/quic-death-spiral-fix/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When &quot;idle&quot; isn&#x27;t idle: how a Linux kernel optimization became a QUIC bug</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>当“空闲”并非空闲：Linux 内核优化如何成为 QUIC 漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48116064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48116064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes how a Linux kernel idle-path optimization inadvertently introduced a QUIC protocol bug. The cross-layer issue illustrates how micro-optimizations can ripple into protocol behavior, prompting a backport or patch to fix. It underscores the need for end-to-end testing when networking stacks evolve.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阐述 Linux 内核空闲路径优化如何无意中引入 QUIC 协议漏洞。此跨层问题揭示了微观优化对协议行为的潜在影响，促使修补和回溯更新。强调在网络栈演进时进行端到端测试的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/42x9UUV0RORhrj931PDGj6/cc8f8122d0bc8aa482d40fa3579e9b5c/When_-idle-_isn-t_idle-_how_a_Linux_kernel_optimization_became_a_QUIC_bug-OG.png' data-full='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/42x9UUV0RORhrj931PDGj6/cc8f8122d0bc8aa482d40fa3579e9b5c/When_-idle-_isn-t_idle-_how_a_Linux_kernel_optimization_became_a_QUIC_bug-OG.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/scientists-id-corkscrew-killer-behind-gruesome-seal-deaths' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cannibalistic attacks between gray seals leave telltale “corkscrew” injuries</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>灰海豹同类相食的螺旋形伤口</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48141960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48141960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scientists have identified corkscrew-shaped injuries on gray seals caused by cannibalistic attacks, revealing a previously underappreciated behavior in crowded colonies. The findings shed light on predator-prey dynamics in crowded colonies and could inform conservation strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科学家在灰海豹身上发现螺旋形咬伤，证实同类之间的攻击行为。研究揭示了拥挤群落中的捕食者–猎物动力学，并可能为保育策略提供参考。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48117810' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Deterministic Fully-Static Whole-Binary Translation Without Heuristics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无需启发式的确定性全静态二进制翻译</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48117810' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48117810' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proposes a deterministic method for fully static binary translation that avoids heuristics entirely. The approach emphasizes reproducibility and predictability in translating whole binaries, which can simplify auditing and cross-architecture research. This work matters for security-sensitive tooling, reproducible research, and robust binary instrumentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出一种无需启发式的确定性全静态二进制翻译方法，强调可重复性和可预测性。通过去除基于经验的决策，改用严格的静态分析或形式方法以实现完整二进制翻译的一致性。这对安全工具、可重复研究和跨架构移植性具有重要意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48113518' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05242' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Beyond Semantic Similarity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>超越语义相似性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48113518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48113518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that semantic similarity alone is not enough to measure language understanding. Proposes richer NLP evaluation tasks—reasoning, world knowledge, and robustness—to reveal true capabilities beyond surface similarity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出超越单纯语义相似性的自然语言处理评估视角，强调需要更丰富的任务来衡量推理、世界知识与鲁棒性。仅靠相似度分数往往掩盖模型的真实理解能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48079202' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://storica.club/blog/troy-was-real/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Real Story of Troy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特洛伊的真实故事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48079202' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48079202' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A historical examination of the Trojan War, separating myth from archaeology and offering context on ancient civilizations, warfare, and culture.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Planet Source Code was an early hub for developers to share snippets and full programs, shaping how open-source culture grew before GitHub existed. The piece recalls community norms, archiving practices, and the nostalgia of pre-GitHub software discovery.</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fc：浮点数据流的无损压缩器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48082586' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48082586' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fc is a lossless compressor for floating-point streams, designed for numerical data pipelines. It targets compressing high-precision signals without sacrificing accuracy, enabling smaller storage and faster transfers in HPC, sensing, or scientific workflows. The project highlights trade-offs between compression ratio and speed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fc 是一个面向浮点数据流的无损压缩器，适用于数值数据管线。它在不损失精度的前提下压缩高精度信号，便于在 HPC、传感以及科学工作流中减少存储和加速传输，同时需要权衡压缩比与速度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f292e2c0c36332dc783df439fcb9f9f4dd74e2ebe2966fb916ab84764566d16b/xtellect/fc' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f292e2c0c36332dc783df439fcb9f9f4dd74e2ebe2966fb916ab84764566d16b/xtellect/fc' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48157229' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(216) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sites.math.rutgers.edu/~zeilberg/Opinion82.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Good Lemma Is Worth a Thousand Theorems (2007)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个好引理胜千个定理（2007）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48157229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48157229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2007 piece argues that a single well-chosen lemma can unlock many theorems, underscoring the value of strategic insight over brute force in math. The article is aimed at mathematicians and students, explaining the craft of finding foundational results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2007 年的一篇文章主张，一条用得恰当的引理往往可以推导出大量定理，强调在数学中对策略性洞见的重视胜过纯粹的证明堆砌。文章面向数学家与学生，阐释发现基础结果的策略性价值。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48166521' data-tags='Design,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grumpywelshman.com/we-are-all-rankers-now-or-why-the-internet-has-turned-to-shit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We Are All Rankers Now: Or Why the Internet Has Turned to Shit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们现在都是排序者：互联网为何变糟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48166521' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 34</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48166521' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>We Are All Rankers Now: Or Why the Internet Has Turned to Shit: The piece argues that ranking-based interfaces and engagement-driven feeds have reshaped the internet, degrading content quality and amplifying divisions. It calls for alternative design principles that de-emphasize popularity metrics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出以投票、排序和参与度驱动的网页设计正在重塑互联网，导致内容质量下降、分化加剧。呼吁采用更少依赖人气指标的替代设计原则。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(218) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mbmccoy.dev/posts/buffons-noodle/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>From Buffon&#x27;s Needle to Buffon&#x27;s Noodle</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从布丰针到布丰面条</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48049343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 33</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48049343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Expands Buffon’s needle idea into an approachable noodle-based experiment, showing how simple probabilistic reasoning can yield elegant estimates for pi. The piece blends math history with hands-on exploration to teach geometry and randomness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将布丰针问题的思路用于用面条进行的直观实验，展示简单的概率推理如何给出对圆周率的优美估算。文章将数学史与动手探究结合，帮助读者理解几何与随机性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(219) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://onlinelibrary.wiley.com/doi/10.1002/ece3.72964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Multi-Species Canopy Latrines in Costa Rican Cloud Forests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>哥斯达黎加云雾林的多物种树冠排便点研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48140368' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48140368' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canopy latrines in Costa Rica&#x27;s cloud forests provide a novel, non-invasive way to sample feces from multiple species. The method supports biodiversity monitoring and disease surveillance in hard-to-reach canopies, with implications for ecology and One Health.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究在哥斯达黎加云雾林中描述树冠排便点，作为一种新颖的非侵入式采样方式，用于同时研究多物种的粪便、生物多样性与疾病监测。该方法显示出可行性，并为生态学和一体化健康研究带来潜在应用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(223) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chreke.com/posts/sql-incorrect-by-construction' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SQL: Incorrect by Construction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SQL：错误来自构造</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48111798' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48111798' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that SQL correctness is hard to guarantee by traditional construction. Encourages correctness-by-construction through safer DSLs, constraints, and invariant tests to harden data workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>认为 SQL 的正确性往往难以通过传统的“按构造写 SQL”方式保证，倡导通过更安全的 DSL、约束和不变量测试实现按构造的正确性，强化数据工作流的安全性。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(229) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.visualcapitalist.com/mapped-americas-most-spoken-languages-after-english-and-spanish/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>America&#x27;s Most-Spoken Languages After English and Spanish</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-title'>(231) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.robinsloan.com/lab/referer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Referer Reality</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Referer 现实：网络追踪与隐私的错觉</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48087643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48087643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the referer header’s real-world behavior and misperceptions around it. The piece argues that relying on Referer for privacy or security is misguided, highlighting inconsistent browser policies and practical data exposure in logs and analytics. It calls for more deliberate data minimization and trust-minimized architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨 Referer 请求头在现实中的表现及常见误解。文章指出将 Referer 作为隐私或安全基础的做法并不可靠，揭示浏览器策略的不一致及日志与分析中暴露的数据。呼吁更严格的数据最小化和信任最小化的架构设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.robinsloan.com/img/moonbound-top-crop-v2.jpg' data-full='https://www.robinsloan.com/img/moonbound-top-crop-v2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>申努尔猜想与 Triton FPSan 的语义</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48156596' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48156596' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores a theoretical connection between Schanuel&#x27;s conjecture from transcendental number theory and the semantics of a formal analysis tool, blending number theory with program analysis concepts. It offers a peek into how deep math can influence semantic reasoning in systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>从数论的申努尔猜想出发，探讨与某类形式分析工具语义之间的潜在联系，将高深的数学思想引入程序分析的语义层面，提供对理论与实现交叉的洞见。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48099625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48099625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Positioned as a European social network centered on verified identities, W aims to curb bots and misinformation while emphasizing privacy and regulatory alignment. The concept highlights ongoing experiments with trusted networks and identity-centric social platforms in Europe.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>W 是一个以已验证身份为核心的欧洲社交网络，旨在减少机器人账户和错误信息，同时强调隐私保护与合规性。该项目反映出在欧洲范围内对可信网络和身份驱动社交平台的探索与讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(234) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://humanpro.co/articles/productivity-isnt-about-going-faster/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Productivity isn&#x27;t about going faster</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>生产力并非来自更快</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48103871' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48103871' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues productivity isn&#x27;t measured by speed but by focus, energy management, and reducing cognitive load. It suggests practical habits and tooling changes for developers to achieve meaningful progress without burning out.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为生产力不是靠更快完成任务，而是通过聚焦、能量管理和降低认知负荷来实现。给出开发者可落地的习惯与工具调整，强调可持续的高效。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(235) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rant.mvh.dev/a-caddy-cert-expired-because-systemd-resolved-was-selectively-broken/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Caddy Cert Expired Because Systemd-Resolved Was Selectively Broken</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>由于 systemd-resolved 的部分故障，Caddy 证书过期</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48101636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48101636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A certificate issued by Caddy expired due to a misbehaving systemd-resolved component, revealing how DNS resolution quirks can break automated TLS workflows. The incident underscores the fragility of TLS automation and the need for robust dependency handling and fallback paths.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>由于 systemd-resolved 的部分故障，Caddy 证书被错误地标记为过期，暴露了 DNS 解析问题对自动化证书管理的影响。此事凸显了 TLS 自动化的脆弱性，以及在依赖管理和兜底路径设计方面的需要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1712797229242-800c3e3298d6?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDN8fGNhZGR5fGVufDB8fHx8MTc3NzkyMjcwN3ww&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' data-full='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1712797229242-800c3e3298d6?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDN8fGNhZGR5fGVufDB8fHx8MTc3NzkyMjcwN3ww&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take on what&#x27;s wrong with AI argues that current systems misalign incentives, overfit on data, and propagate biases while hype outpaces practical safety. It calls for clearer evaluation, accountability, and safer deployment practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇观点文章质疑 AI 的现状，指出系统在激励机制、数据偏差和可解释性方面的不足，以及行业对安全与可控性的高涨热情与现实挑战之间的矛盾。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48175637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 9</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48175637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Étienne Ghys traces the evolution of letter shapes across art and math, linking Leonardo da Vinci’s sketches to Donald Knuth’s typographic work. The talk reveals how visual form intersects with computation and design, relevant to typography, UI, and programming culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>埃蒂安·盖伊斯追溯字母形状在艺术与数学中的演变，将达·芬奇的素描与唐纳德·克努斯的排版工作联系起来。该讲座揭示了视觉形式、计算与设计之间的交叉，对排版、用户界面与编程文化具有启示作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(249) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ABGEO/mezz' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Mezz, a curl-able WiFi sandbox for IoT pentesting</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48149461' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 8</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48149461' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mezz is a curl-friendly, portable WiFi sandbox for IoT pentesting that lets researchers script network scenarios and sandbox device behavior. It lowers the friction for reproducible security testing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mezz 是一个 curl 友好、便携的物联网 WiFi 沙箱，面向渗透测试。研究者可以脚本化网络场景并模拟设备行为，从而实现可重复的安全测试，降低门槛。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.inc.com/connor-jewiss/ai-wearables-are-coming-but-theyll-need-to-pass-this-crucial-coffee-shop-test-to-survive/91345526' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Wearables Are Coming but They&#x27;ll Need to Pass the Coffee Shop Test to Survive</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 穿戴设备来袭，但要通过咖啡馆测试才算成功</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48173501' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 5</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48173501' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI wearables are coming, but to survive in real life they&#x27;ll need to pass the coffee-shop test: respect privacy, perform on-device processing, and avoid being disruptive in public spaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 穿戴设备即将上市，但要在日常生活中获得广泛采用，需要通过“咖啡馆测试”：确保隐私保护、本地处理和在公共场合的低干扰性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(254) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://la-machine.fr/en?market=us&amp;_redirected=1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>La Machine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>La Machine</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48169989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 3</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48169989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>La Machine is a monumental mechanical creature or installation; the piece covers its engineering feats, public art impact, and the intersection of robotics with culture. It highlights how large-scale automata captivate audiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>La Machine 指的是一件宏大机械装置或公共艺术作品，文章聚焦其工程成就、对观众的影响，以及机器人技术如何与文化交汇。</p>
